fable-5.1/02-bug-fixing/RESPONSE.md

Task 02 — TTL/LRU cache bug fix

Files changed (all inside this directory):

  • ttl_cache.py — fixed implementation, public API unchanged (TTLCache(max_size, ttl_seconds, clock=time.monotonic), put, get, __len__, attributes max_size, ttl_seconds, clock, _items).
  • test_ttl_cache.py — the three original tests kept verbatim, 22 regression tests added.

Baseline before the fix: python3 -m unittest -v test_ttl_cache -> Ran 3 tests ... FAILED (failures=2) (test_expires_at_boundary, test_get_refreshes_lru).

Root causes

# Where Bug Spec bullet violated
1 get if not value: raise KeyError(key) — any falsy stored value (0, False, None, "", []) was reported as missing. get returns falsy values
2 put Timestamps were taken from wall-clock time.time(), but get compared them against the injected self.clock(). Two different time bases: with the test clock at 100.0 the age is hugely negative, so nothing ever expired; with the real default clock the comparison is between monotonic() and epoch seconds and is meaningless either way. TTL uses the injected monotonic clock
3 get Expiry test was age > ttl_seconds; an entry with age == ttl_seconds was still served. expires at age >= ttl_seconds
4 get Never called move_to_end, so the OrderedDict order stayed insertion order and eviction was FIFO, not LRU. successful get refreshes recency
5 put Re-assigning an existing key in an OrderedDict keeps its old position, so an update did not become most-recently-used. put updates recency
6 put Capacity check ran before looking at whether the key already existed, so updating a key in a full cache evicted an unrelated live entry (the key being updated already owned a slot). put updates an existing key
7 put Expired entries were never purged, so they kept occupying slots and a live LRU entry was evicted to make room while dead entries stayed. expired entries removed before enforcing capacity; eviction removes the LRU live entry
8 get / __len__ An expired entry raised KeyError but stayed in _items, so it was counted by len() and still took up capacity. (consequence of 7)
9 __init__ No validation: TTLCache(0, ...), TTLCache(-1, ...), TTLCache(n, 0), TTLCache(n, -1) were accepted. max_size < 1 / ttl_seconds <= 0 raise ValueError

Fixes

  • __init__: raise ValueError for max_size < 1 and for ttl_seconds <= 0.
  • Single time source: every timestamp and every age check now uses self.clock(); time is only referenced for the time.monotonic default. Added private helpers _is_expired(stored_at, now) (now - stored_at >= ttl_seconds, i.e. >= at the boundary) and _purge_expired(now).
  • put(key, value):
    1. read the clock once, purge every expired entry (this also drops the key itself if it had expired);
    2. if the key is still present it is live: overwrite (value, now) and move_to_end — value, timestamp and recency all refreshed, and nothing is evicted because no new slot is needed;
    3. otherwise, if the cache is at capacity, popitem(last=False) evicts the least recently used entry — which after the purge is guaranteed to be a live one — then insert at the MRU end.
  • get(key): KeyError propagates naturally for a missing key; an expired entry is deleted and re-raised as KeyError; a hit calls move_to_end (recency) but leaves the stored timestamp untouched (TTL is not refreshed). The falsy-value check is gone — the value is returned as stored.
  • __len__: purges expired entries first, so len(cache) is the number of live entries and is consistent with what get would actually serve (same convention as cachetools.TTLCache).

Design note: because a get moves an entry to the MRU end without changing its timestamp, the LRU order is not a timestamp order, so the purge has to scan all entries (O(n) per put/len). That is the cost of the "recency is refreshed, TTL is not" rule and is fine at the sizes this class is meant for; a heap keyed on expiry could make it O(log n) if ever needed, without changing the public API.

Regression cases added (test_ttl_cache.py)

All 22 new tests use the injected Clock (starting at 100.0) so time is fully deterministic. "Fails on original" says whether the test catches the original faulty implementation (verified by running the final suite against a copy of the original file: 15 failures + 8 errors, 25 tests).

fable-5.1/02-bug-fixing/TASK.md

Task 02 — Bug fixing: TTL/LRU cache

ttl_cache.py is a deliberately faulty implementation. Fix it without changing the public API and add regression tests.

Required behavior:

  • get(key) returns the stored value, including falsy values such as 0, False and None; it raises KeyError only for missing or expired entries.
  • TTL uses the injected monotonic clock and expires at age >= ttl_seconds.
  • A successful get refreshes LRU recency but does not refresh TTL.
  • put updates an existing key's value, timestamp and recency.
  • Before enforcing capacity, expired entries are removed.
  • Capacity eviction removes the least recently used live entry.
  • max_size < 1 and ttl_seconds <= 0 raise ValueError.

Run the tests. Write RESPONSE.md with the root causes, fixes, regression cases, and exact test result. Work only in this directory.

fable-5.1/02-bug-fixing/test_ttl_cache.py

import unittest
from unittest import mock

import ttl_cache
from ttl_cache import TTLCache


class Clock:
    def __init__(self):
        self.now = 100.0

    def __call__(self):
        return self.now


class TTLCacheTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.clock = Clock()

    # --- original tests -------------------------------------------------

    def test_round_trip(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")

    def test_expires_at_boundary(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 10
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_get_refreshes_lru(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.get("a")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")

    # --- regression: falsy values and missing keys ----------------------

    def test_falsy_values_are_returned(self):
        cache = TTLCache(10, 10, self.clock)
        falsy = {"zero": 0, "false": False, "none": None, "empty": "", "list": []}
        for key, value in falsy.items():
            cache.put(key, value)
        for key, value in falsy.items():
            with self.subTest(key=key):
                self.assertEqual(cache.get(key), value)
        self.assertIs(cache.get("false"), False)
        self.assertIsNone(cache.get("none"))

    def test_missing_key_raises_key_error(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("missing")

    # --- regression: TTL uses the injected clock only -------------------

    def test_uses_injected_clock_not_wall_clock(self):
        class NoTimeModule:
            @staticmethod
            def time():
                raise AssertionError("time.time() must not be used")

            @staticmethod
            def monotonic():
                raise AssertionError("time.monotonic() must not be used when a clock is injected")

        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        with mock.patch.object(ttl_cache, "time", NoTimeModule):
            cache.put("a", "A")
            self.clock.now += 9
            self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
            self.clock.now += 1
            with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
                cache.get("a")
            self.assertEqual(len(cache), 0)

    def test_live_just_before_ttl(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 9.999
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")

    def test_fractional_ttl(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 0.5, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 0.25
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.clock.now += 0.25
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_expired_get_purges_entry(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 10
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 0)
        self.assertNotIn("a", cache._items)

    # --- regression: get refreshes recency, not TTL ---------------------

    def test_get_does_not_refresh_ttl(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 6
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.clock.now += 4  # age is 10 since put, only 4 since the last get
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    # --- regression: put on an existing key -----------------------------

    def test_put_updates_value(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", 1)
        cache.put("a", 2)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 1)

    def test_put_refreshes_ttl(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A1")
        self.clock.now += 6
        cache.put("a", "A2")
        self.clock.now += 6  # 12s since first put, 6s since the update
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")
        self.clock.now += 4  # 10s since the update
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_put_refreshes_lru(self):
        # Cache is not full when "a" is updated, so only recency is at play:
        # the original code left "a" in its old (LRU) slot in this case.
        cache = TTLCache(3, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("a", "A2")  # "a" becomes most recently used
        cache.put("c", "C")
        cache.put("d", "D")  # evicts "b", the least recently used
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("d"), "D")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")

    def test_put_existing_key_when_full_does_not_evict(self):
        # Update the most recently used key: the original code evicted the
        # unrelated LRU entry "a" to make room for a key that already had a slot.
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("b", "B2")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B2")
        # And the same when the updated key is the LRU one.
        cache.put("a", "A2")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B2")

    def test_put_on_expired_key_reinserts(self):
        cache = TTLCache(1, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 10
        cache.put("a", "A2")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 1)

    # --- regression: expired entries are removed before capacity ---------

    def test_expired_entries_removed_before_capacity_enforced(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        self.clock.now += 10  # both expired
        cache.put("c", "C")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 1)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")

    def test_expired_mru_entry_is_purged_instead_of_evicting_live_lru(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")  # stored at t=100
        cache.put("b", "B")  # stored at t=100
        self.clock.now += 5
        cache.put("b", "B2")  # "b" stored at t=105
        cache.get("a")  # "a" is now most recently used but still stamped t=100
        self.clock.now += 5  # t=110: "a" expired (age 10), "b" live (age 5)
        cache.put("c", "C")
        # The live LRU entry "b" must survive; the expired MRU entry "a" goes.
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B2")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)

    # --- regression: capacity eviction is LRU ---------------------------

    def test_capacity_evicts_least_recently_used(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)

    def test_capacity_one(self):
        cache = TTLCache(1, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 1)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_len_never_exceeds_max_size(self):
        cache = TTLCache(3, 10, self.clock)
        for i in range(20):
            cache.put(i, i)
            self.assertLessEqual(len(cache), 3)
        self.assertEqual(sorted(cache._items), [17, 18, 19])

    # --- regression: len excludes expired entries -----------------------

    def test_len_excludes_expired(self):
        cache = TTLCache(3, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 5
        cache.put("b", "B")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)
        self.clock.now += 5  # "a" expired, "b" live
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 1)
        self.clock.now += 5  # both expired
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 0)

    # --- regression: constructor validation -----------------------------

    def test_invalid_max_size_raises(self):
        for bad in (0, -1):
            with self.subTest(max_size=bad):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    TTLCache(bad, 10, self.clock)

    def test_invalid_ttl_raises(self):
        for bad in (0, -1, -0.5):
            with self.subTest(ttl_seconds=bad):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    TTLCache(2, bad, self.clock)

    def test_valid_boundaries_accepted(self):
        TTLCache(1, 0.001, self.clock)
        TTLCache(1, 1, self.clock)

    def test_default_clock_round_trip(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 60)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

fable-5.1/02-bug-fixing/ttl_cache.py

import time
from collections import OrderedDict


class TTLCache:
    """Bounded LRU cache whose entries also expire ``ttl_seconds`` after they
    were stored.

    ``clock`` is a zero-argument callable returning seconds from a monotonic
    source. It is the *only* time source the cache uses, which keeps behaviour
    deterministic under test and immune to wall-clock jumps.
    """

    def __init__(self, max_size, ttl_seconds, clock=time.monotonic):
        if max_size < 1:
            raise ValueError(f"max_size must be >= 1, got {max_size!r}")
        if ttl_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"ttl_seconds must be > 0, got {ttl_seconds!r}")
        self.max_size = max_size
        self.ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
        self.clock = clock
        # key -> (value, stored_at), ordered from least to most recently used.
        self._items = OrderedDict()

    def _is_expired(self, stored_at, now):
        return now - stored_at >= self.ttl_seconds

    def _purge_expired(self, now):
        expired = [
            key
            for key, (_, stored_at) in self._items.items()
            if self._is_expired(stored_at, now)
        ]
        for key in expired:
            del self._items[key]

    def put(self, key, value):
        now = self.clock()
        # Free slots held by dead entries before deciding whether to evict.
        self._purge_expired(now)
        if key in self._items:
            # Existing live key: new value, fresh TTL, most recently used.
            # It already owns a slot, so nothing else is evicted.
            self._items[key] = (value, now)
            self._items.move_to_end(key)
            return
        if len(self._items) >= self.max_size:
            # Evict the least recently used live entry.
            self._items.popitem(last=False)
        self._items[key] = (value, now)

    def get(self, key):
        value, stored_at = self._items[key]  # KeyError for a missing key
        if self._is_expired(stored_at, self.clock()):
            del self._items[key]
            raise KeyError(key)
        # A hit refreshes recency only; the stored timestamp is untouched, so
        # the TTL keeps counting from when the value was put.
        self._items.move_to_end(key)
        return value

    def __len__(self):
        self._purge_expired(self.clock())
        return len(self._items)

Evaluation 9.75 / 10 graded blind as submission S

Correct fix that purges only when at capacity, which meets 'before enforcing capacity' exactly; len() then over-counts expired entries. Tests miss update recency.

  • Lazy purge is cheaper and still meets the brief

  • Fails other suites (K,T,U,Y) that require purging below capacity, all observed through len()

gemini-3.1-pro/02-bug-fixing/RESPONSE.md

Response — Bug Fixing: TTL/LRU Cache

1. Root Causes

  1. Incorrect Clock Usage on Insertion (time.time() vs self.clock()): In TTLCache.put(), items were stored with time.time() instead of self.clock(). When custom clocks or monotonic clocks were injected, calculating expiration via self.clock() - created resulted in invalid ages (often negative or wildly inaccurate values), breaking TTL expiration.

  2. Expiration Boundary Condition (> vs >=): In TTLCache.get(), expiration checked self.clock() - created > self.ttl_seconds. The specification requires items to expire at age >= ttl_seconds. Items at the exact boundary (age == ttl_seconds) failed to expire.

  3. Rejection of Falsy Values: In TTLCache.get(), the check if not value: raise KeyError(key) caused legitimate stored falsy values (e.g. 0, False, None, "", [], {}) to incorrectly raise KeyError.

  4. Failure to Refresh LRU Recency on get(): In TTLCache.get(), accessing an entry did not update its position in the internal OrderedDict. As a result, items read frequently remained at their insertion position and were prematurely evicted as the least recently used entries.

  5. Existing Keys in put() Triggering Unnecessary Eviction and Failing to Update Recency: In TTLCache.put(), capacity was checked before verifying whether key was already present in self._items. If the cache was full, updating an existing key evicted the LRU item even though cache size did not grow. Furthermore, reassigning an existing key in OrderedDict without move_to_end() failed to update the key's recency to MRU.

  6. Capacity Eviction Without Prior Expired Entry Removal: TTLCache.put() performed popitem(last=False) directly upon reaching capacity without first removing expired entries. This caused active, unexpired entries to be evicted even when expired entries were present in the cache.

  7. Missing Constructor Parameter Validation: TTLCache.__init__() did not validate max_size and ttl_seconds. Parameters violating max_size < 1 or ttl_seconds <= 0 were silently accepted instead of raising ValueError.

2. Fixes

  1. Constructor Validation: Added validation in __init__:

    if max_size < 1:
    raise ValueError(f"max_size must be >= 1, got {max_size}")
if ttl_seconds <= 0:
    raise ValueError(f"ttl_seconds must be > 0, got {ttl_seconds}")

  2. Injected Clock & Boundary Checks: Used now = self.clock() in put() when recording item creation timestamps. In get(), expired entries are identified using self.clock() - created >= self.ttl_seconds, deleted from self._items, and a KeyError is raised.

  3. Falsy Values Handling: Removed if not value: raise KeyError(key) from get(), checking instead if key not in self._items: so all valid values (including 0, False, None) are returned properly.

  4. LRU Recency Refresh on get(): Called self._items.move_to_end(key) on successful get(). Stored timestamps remain untouched, preserving TTL while refreshing LRU recency.

  5. put() Existing Key Handling: When key in self._items, put() updates self._items[key] = (value, now) and calls self._items.move_to_end(key), returning immediately without evicting any entries.

  6. Expired Entries Removal Before Capacity Eviction: Implemented _purge_expired(now) to delete all entries with now - created >= self.ttl_seconds. When adding a new key at capacity (len(self._items) >= self.max_size), _purge_expired(now) is invoked first. Only if len(self._items) >= self.max_size remains true is the LRU live entry evicted via self._items.popitem(last=False).

3. Regression Test Cases Added

The following regression test cases were added to test_ttl_cache.py:

  • test_falsy_values_returned: Verifies that 0, False, None, "", [], and {} are stored and retrieved without raising KeyError.
  • test_missing_key_raises_key_error: Verifies that querying nonexistent keys raises KeyError.
  • test_invalid_parameters_raise_value_error: Verifies that max_size < 1 (0, -1) and ttl_seconds <= 0 (0, -5) raise ValueError.
  • test_valid_boundary_parameters: Verifies that boundary inputs (max_size=1, ttl_seconds=0.001) initialize correctly.
  • test_ttl_expiration_just_before_and_at_boundary: Verifies entry is valid at age = 9.999 and expires at age = 10.0 for ttl_seconds = 10.
  • test_get_does_not_refresh_ttl: Verifies that get() updates LRU recency without resetting or extending TTL.
  • test_put_updates_existing_key: Verifies that updating an existing key updates its value, resets its TTL timestamp, and refreshes recency without evicting other entries.
  • test_expired_entries_removed_before_capacity_enforcement: Verifies that when capacity is reached, expired entries are removed first so live entries are not evicted.
  • test_capacity_eviction_removes_least_recently_used_live_entry: Verifies that when all entries are live and capacity is reached, the least recently used live entry is evicted.
  • test_put_existing_key_does_not_evict_other_keys: Verifies that updating an existing key at full capacity does not evict another key.
  • test_default_clock_instantiation: Verifies instantiation and basic operations using default time.monotonic.

gemini-3.1-pro/02-bug-fixing/TASK.md

Task 02 — Bug fixing: TTL/LRU cache

ttl_cache.py is a deliberately faulty implementation. Fix it without changing the public API and add regression tests.

Required behavior:

  • get(key) returns the stored value, including falsy values such as 0, False and None; it raises KeyError only for missing or expired entries.
  • TTL uses the injected monotonic clock and expires at age >= ttl_seconds.
  • A successful get refreshes LRU recency but does not refresh TTL.
  • put updates an existing key's value, timestamp and recency.
  • Before enforcing capacity, expired entries are removed.
  • Capacity eviction removes the least recently used live entry.
  • max_size < 1 and ttl_seconds <= 0 raise ValueError.

Run the tests. Write RESPONSE.md with the root causes, fixes, regression cases, and exact test result. Work only in this directory.

gemini-3.1-pro/02-bug-fixing/test_ttl_cache.py

import unittest

from ttl_cache import TTLCache


class Clock:
    def __init__(self):
        self.now = 100.0

    def __call__(self):
        return self.now


class TTLCacheTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.clock = Clock()

    def test_round_trip(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")

    def test_expires_at_boundary(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 10
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_get_refreshes_lru(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.get("a")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")

    def test_falsy_values_returned(self):
        cache = TTLCache(5, 10, self.clock)
        test_cases = [
            ("zero", 0),
            ("false", False),
            ("none", None),
            ("empty_str", ""),
            ("empty_list", []),
            ("empty_dict", {}),
        ]
        for key, value in test_cases:
            cache.put(key, value)
            self.assertEqual(cache.get(key), value)

    def test_missing_key_raises_key_error(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("nonexistent")

    def test_invalid_parameters_raise_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(0, 10, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(-1, 10, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(2, 0, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(2, -5, self.clock)

    def test_valid_boundary_parameters(self):
        cache = TTLCache(1, 0.001, self.clock)
        self.assertEqual(cache.max_size, 1)
        self.assertEqual(cache.ttl_seconds, 0.001)

    def test_ttl_expiration_just_before_and_at_boundary(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 9.999
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.clock.now += 0.001  # age == 10.0
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_get_does_not_refresh_ttl(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 5
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")  # accesses at t=105
        self.clock.now += 5  # now t=110, age is 10 >= 10
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_put_updates_existing_key(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        self.clock.now += 5
        # Update existing key 'a': should update value, timestamp (t=105), and recency
        cache.put("a", "A_updated")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A_updated")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)

        # Advance to t=112: 'b' age is 12 (expired), 'a' age is 7 (live)
        self.clock.now += 7
        cache.put("c", "C")  # 'b' should be removed as expired, 'a' remains
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A_updated")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")

    def test_expired_entries_removed_before_capacity_enforcement(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")  # t=100
        self.clock.now += 5
        cache.put("b", "B")  # t=105
        self.clock.now += 1
        cache.get("a")       # t=106, LRU order is now [b, a]

        # Advance to t=110: 'a' age is 10 (expired), 'b' age is 5 (live)
        self.clock.now += 4
        # Adding 'c' triggers capacity check. 'a' is expired and must be removed
        # instead of evicting the least recently used live entry ('b')
        cache.put("c", "C")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_capacity_eviction_removes_least_recently_used_live_entry(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")  # t=100
        self.clock.now += 1
        cache.put("b", "B")  # t=101
        self.clock.now += 1  # t=102: both live
        cache.put("c", "C")  # capacity full, 'a' is LRU live entry -> evicted
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")

    def test_put_existing_key_does_not_evict_other_keys(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("b", "B_new")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B_new")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)

    def test_default_clock_instantiation(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10)
        cache.put("x", 42)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("x"), 42)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

gemini-3.1-pro/02-bug-fixing/ttl_cache.py

import time
from collections import OrderedDict


class TTLCache:
    def __init__(self, max_size, ttl_seconds, clock=time.monotonic):
        if max_size < 1:
            raise ValueError(f"max_size must be >= 1, got {max_size}")
        if ttl_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"ttl_seconds must be > 0, got {ttl_seconds}")
        self.max_size = max_size
        self.ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
        self.clock = clock
        self._items = OrderedDict()

    def _purge_expired(self, now=None):
        if now is None:
            now = self.clock()
        expired = [k for k, (_, created) in self._items.items() if now - created >= self.ttl_seconds]
        for k in expired:
            del self._items[k]

    def put(self, key, value):
        now = self.clock()
        if key in self._items:
            self._items[key] = (value, now)
            self._items.move_to_end(key)
            return

        if len(self._items) >= self.max_size:
            self._purge_expired(now)

        if len(self._items) >= self.max_size:
            self._items.popitem(last=False)

        self._items[key] = (value, now)

    def get(self, key):
        if key not in self._items:
            raise KeyError(key)
        value, created = self._items[key]
        if self.clock() - created >= self.ttl_seconds:
            del self._items[key]
            raise KeyError(key)
        self._items.move_to_end(key)
        return value

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self._items)

gemini-3.8-flash/02-bug-fixing/RESPONSE.md

Task 02 — Bug Fixing: TTL/LRU Cache Response

1. Root Causes

  1. Clock Injection and Monotonic Mismatch in put: put recorded timestamps using time.time() (wall-clock) instead of self.clock() (injected monotonic clock). This caused expiration calculations in get (which evaluated self.clock() - created) to compute negative or erroneous durations when an injected clock was provided.

  2. Incorrect Expiration Boundary Condition: get checked self.clock() - created > self.ttl_seconds. The specification requires expiration at age >= ttl_seconds. Consequently, entries at the exact expiration boundary (age == ttl_seconds) failed to expire.

  3. Falsy Values Rejected in get: get included if not value: raise KeyError(key), causing legitimate stored falsy values (0, False, None, empty string, empty containers) to erroneously raise KeyError.

  4. Missing LRU Recency Refresh on get: A successful get did not update key recency in self._items via move_to_end(key). The accessed entry remained at its original position, leading to premature eviction under subsequent put calls.

  5. Improper Existing Key Updates in put: When put updated an existing key:

    • OrderedDict does not update the position on key assignment, so the key's recency was not refreshed.
    • If len(self._items) >= self.max_size, put blindly popped an item even though updating an existing key does not increase dictionary size.

  6. Capacity Eviction Without Expired Entry Removal: put performed capacity eviction by popping the oldest item before checking or removing expired items. This could evict a live item while expired items remained in the cache.

  7. Missing Constructor Validation: TTLCache.__init__ did not validate max_size or ttl_seconds, failing to raise ValueError when max_size < 1 or ttl_seconds <= 0.

2. Fixes

  1. Parameter Validation: Added checks in __init__:

    • if max_size < 1: raise ValueError(...)
    • if ttl_seconds <= 0: raise ValueError(...)

  2. Consistent Clock & Expiration:

    • put now records now = self.clock() as the creation timestamp.
    • Expiration condition updated to now - created >= self.ttl_seconds.
    • Expired items accessed in get are deleted from _items before raising KeyError.

  3. Support for Falsy Values: Removed if not value: check in get. KeyError is raised only when a key is absent or expired.

  4. Recency Maintenance:

    • get: Calls self._items.move_to_end(key) upon successful retrieval without modifying the timestamp.
    • put: Updates (value, now) and calls self._items.move_to_end(key) for existing keys immediately, without evicting any items.

  5. Expired Entry Removal Prior to Eviction: Implemented helper method _purge_expired(now). When adding a new key at capacity (len(self._items) >= self.max_size), _purge_expired purges expired entries first. If capacity is still saturated, popitem(last=False) removes the least recently used live entry.

  6. Accurate Length Counting: __len__ invokes _purge_expired() so len(cache) reflects only unexpired, accessible entries.

3. Regression Cases

The following test suite in test_ttl_cache.py verifies all requirements:

  • test_round_trip: Verifies insertion and retrieval of a key-value pair.
  • test_expires_at_boundary: Verifies that an entry expires at the exact boundary age == ttl_seconds.
  • test_get_refreshes_lru: Verifies that a successful get refreshes LRU recency, protecting the accessed key from capacity eviction.
  • test_get_before_boundary: Verifies that an entry remains valid just before the expiration threshold (age < ttl_seconds).
  • test_get_missing_key_raises_key_error: Verifies that querying a non-existent key raises KeyError.
  • test_falsy_values_returned: Verifies that falsy values (0, False, None, "", [], {}, ()) are stored and returned without raising KeyError.
  • test_get_does_not_refresh_ttl: Verifies that calling get does not reset the entry's creation timestamp or prolong its TTL.
  • test_put_updates_existing_key_value: Verifies that re-putting an existing key updates its value.
  • test_put_updates_existing_key_timestamp: Verifies that re-putting an existing key resets its TTL timestamp.
  • test_put_updates_existing_key_recency: Verifies that re-putting an existing key updates its recency to MRU.
  • test_put_existing_key_does_not_exceed_capacity: Verifies that updating an existing key at capacity does not evict other entries.
  • test_expired_entries_removed_before_enforcing_capacity: Verifies that expired entries are purged prior to capacity enforcement, ensuring an LRU live entry is preserved when an expired entry can be discarded instead.
  • test_capacity_eviction_removes_lru_live_entry: Verifies that when all entries are live, capacity eviction evicts the least recently used entry.
  • test_invalid_max_size_raises_value_error: Verifies that max_size < 1 (e.g. 0, -1) raises ValueError.
  • test_invalid_ttl_seconds_raises_value_error: Verifies that ttl_seconds <= 0 (e.g. 0, -5) raises ValueError.
  • test_default_clock_used_when_none_provided: Verifies that omitting the clock argument defaults to time.monotonic.
  • test_len_behavior: Verifies that len(cache) reflects live entries and excludes expired items.

gemini-3.8-flash/02-bug-fixing/TASK.md

Task 02 — Bug fixing: TTL/LRU cache

ttl_cache.py is a deliberately faulty implementation. Fix it without changing the public API and add regression tests.

Required behavior:

  • get(key) returns the stored value, including falsy values such as 0, False and None; it raises KeyError only for missing or expired entries.
  • TTL uses the injected monotonic clock and expires at age >= ttl_seconds.
  • A successful get refreshes LRU recency but does not refresh TTL.
  • put updates an existing key's value, timestamp and recency.
  • Before enforcing capacity, expired entries are removed.
  • Capacity eviction removes the least recently used live entry.
  • max_size < 1 and ttl_seconds <= 0 raise ValueError.

Run the tests. Write RESPONSE.md with the root causes, fixes, regression cases, and exact test result. Work only in this directory.

gemini-3.8-flash/02-bug-fixing/test_ttl_cache.py

import unittest

from ttl_cache import TTLCache


class Clock:
    def __init__(self):
        self.now = 100.0

    def __call__(self):
        return self.now


class TTLCacheTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.clock = Clock()

    def test_round_trip(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")

    def test_expires_at_boundary(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 10
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_get_refreshes_lru(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.get("a")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")

    def test_get_before_boundary(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 9.999
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")

    def test_get_missing_key_raises_key_error(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("nonexistent")

    def test_falsy_values_returned(self):
        falsy_cases = [0, False, None, "", [], {}, ()]
        cache = TTLCache(len(falsy_cases), 10, self.clock)
        for i, val in enumerate(falsy_cases):
            cache.put(f"key_{i}", val)
        for i, val in enumerate(falsy_cases):
            self.assertEqual(cache.get(f"key_{i}"), val)

    def test_get_does_not_refresh_ttl(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 6.0
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")  # Recency refreshed, but TTL should NOT be refreshed
        self.clock.now += 4.0  # Total age is now 10.0 >= 10.0
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_put_updates_existing_key_value(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "initial")
        cache.put("a", "updated")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "updated")

    def test_put_updates_existing_key_timestamp(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "v1")  # created at 100.0
        self.clock.now += 8.0  # at 108.0
        cache.put("a", "v2")  # timestamp updated to 108.0
        self.clock.now += 5.0  # at 113.0 (age from first put is 13, but age from second is 5)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "v2")
        self.clock.now += 5.0  # at 118.0 (age from second put is 10.0 >= 10.0)
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_put_updates_existing_key_recency(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        # 'a' is LRU. Updating 'a' via put should make 'b' LRU.
        cache.put("a", "A2")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        # 'b' should be evicted, 'a' and 'c' should remain
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")

    def test_put_existing_key_does_not_exceed_capacity(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("b", "B_new")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B_new")

    def test_expired_entries_removed_before_enforcing_capacity(self):
        # Cache max_size=2, ttl=10
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")  # t=100
        self.clock.now += 2.0  # t=102
        cache.put("b", "B")  # t=102
        self.clock.now += 2.0  # t=104
        cache.get("a")  # 'a' recency refreshed -> order is ['b', 'a'], so 'b' is LRU
        self.clock.now += 7.0  # t=111: 'a' age is 11 >= 10 (expired), 'b' age is 9 < 10 (live)

        # Before enforcing capacity, 'a' is removed because it's expired.
        # 'b' (which is live, even though it was the LRU entry) must NOT be evicted!
        cache.put("c", "C")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_capacity_eviction_removes_lru_live_entry(self):
        cache = TTLCache(3, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        cache.get("a")  # order becomes b, c, a
        cache.put("d", "D")  # evicts 'b'
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("d"), "D")

    def test_invalid_max_size_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(0, 10, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(-1, 10, self.clock)

    def test_invalid_ttl_seconds_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(2, 0, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(2, -5, self.clock)

    def test_default_clock_used_when_none_provided(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 100)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")

    def test_len_behavior(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 0)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 1)
        self.clock.now += 10
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

gemini-3.8-flash/02-bug-fixing/ttl_cache.py

import time
from collections import OrderedDict


class TTLCache:
    def __init__(self, max_size, ttl_seconds, clock=time.monotonic):
        if max_size < 1:
            raise ValueError(f"max_size must be >= 1, got {max_size}")
        if ttl_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"ttl_seconds must be > 0, got {ttl_seconds}")
        self.max_size = max_size
        self.ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
        self.clock = clock
        self._items = OrderedDict()

    def _purge_expired(self, now=None):
        if now is None:
            now = self.clock()
        expired = [
            k for k, (_, created) in self._items.items()
            if now - created >= self.ttl_seconds
        ]
        for k in expired:
            del self._items[k]

    def put(self, key, value):
        now = self.clock()
        if key in self._items:
            self._items[key] = (value, now)
            self._items.move_to_end(key)
            return

        if len(self._items) >= self.max_size:
            self._purge_expired(now)

        while len(self._items) >= self.max_size:
            self._items.popitem(last=False)

        self._items[key] = (value, now)

    def get(self, key):
        value, created = self._items[key]
        now = self.clock()
        if now - created >= self.ttl_seconds:
            del self._items[key]
            raise KeyError(key)
        self._items.move_to_end(key)
        return value

    def __len__(self):
        self._purge_expired()
        return len(self._items)

muse-wonder/02-bug-fixing/RESPONSE.md

Bug fixing: TTL/LRU cache — Response

Root causes

  1. Wrong clock in put: put stored time.time() (wall clock) while get compared against the injected self.clock() (monotonic/fake clock). Ages were computed across two different epochs, so entries effectively never expired with an injected clock.
  2. Expiry boundary off by one: get used age > ttl_seconds instead of age >= ttl_seconds, so an entry aged exactly ttl_seconds was still returned.
  3. Falsy values rejected: get contained if not value: raise KeyError(key), so stored 0, False, None, and "" were incorrectly reported as missing.
  4. get did not refresh LRU recency: get returned the value without moving the key to the most-recently-used end of the OrderedDict, so a recently read entry could be evicted next.
  5. put on an existing key evicted and did not refresh recency: put unconditionally evicted the LRU entry when at capacity — even when merely updating an existing key — and assigning to an existing OrderedDict key does not change its position, so the updated key kept its old recency. (Its timestamp was also stored with the wrong clock, per cause 1.)
  6. Expired entries not purged before capacity enforcement: put evicted the LRU entry without first removing expired entries, so a live LRU entry could be evicted while expired entries still occupied capacity.
  7. No argument validation: the constructor accepted max_size < 1 and ttl_seconds <= 0 without raising ValueError.

Fixes (ttl_cache.py, public API unchanged)

  • Constructor now raises ValueError for max_size < 1 and for ttl_seconds <= 0.
  • Added a _purge_expired(now) helper that deletes every entry with now - created >= ttl_seconds.
  • put now timestamps with self.clock(), purges expired entries before enforcing capacity, updates an existing key's value/timestamp and moves it to most-recently-used without evicting, and otherwise evicts least-recently-used live entries (popitem(last=False)) until there is room.
  • get now raises KeyError only for missing or expired keys (deleting the expired entry), uses age >= ttl_seconds, moves a successful hit to most-recently-used, does not touch the stored timestamp (so TTL is not refreshed), and returns the value as stored, including falsy values.
  • __len__ purges expired entries before counting, so it reports live entries.

Regression cases (added to test_ttl_cache.py)

The 3 pre-existing tests were kept; 10 regression tests were added:

  • test_falsy_values_are_returned — 0, False, None, "" round-trip through get.
  • test_missing_key_raises_key_error — KeyError only for an absent key.
  • test_expired_entry_raises_and_is_removed — expired get raises KeyError and len drops to 0.
  • test_get_does_not_refresh_ttl — a hit at age 6 does not prevent expiry at age 11 with TTL 10.
  • test_put_updates_value_timestamp_and_recency — re-put replaces the value, restarts TTL, and makes the key most-recently-used so the other entry is evicted.
  • test_put_existing_key_does_not_evict — updating at capacity keeps both entries (len == 2).
  • test_expired_entries_removed_before_capacity_eviction — expired MRU entry is purged so the live LRU entry survives an insert at capacity.
  • test_capacity_evicts_lru_live_entry — plain LRU eviction among live entries.
  • test_invalid_max_size_raises_value_error — max_size of 0 and -1.
  • test_invalid_ttl_raises_value_error — ttl_seconds of 0 and -5.

Before the fix, the pre-existing suite failed 2 of 3 tests (test_expires_at_boundary and test_get_refreshes_lru: AssertionError: KeyError not raised), confirming causes 1, 2, and 4.

muse-wonder/02-bug-fixing/TASK.md

Task 02 — Bug fixing: TTL/LRU cache

ttl_cache.py is a deliberately faulty implementation. Fix it without changing the public API and add regression tests.

Required behavior:

  • get(key) returns the stored value, including falsy values such as 0, False and None; it raises KeyError only for missing or expired entries.
  • TTL uses the injected monotonic clock and expires at age >= ttl_seconds.
  • A successful get refreshes LRU recency but does not refresh TTL.
  • put updates an existing key's value, timestamp and recency.
  • Before enforcing capacity, expired entries are removed.
  • Capacity eviction removes the least recently used live entry.
  • max_size < 1 and ttl_seconds <= 0 raise ValueError.

Run the tests. Write RESPONSE.md with the root causes, fixes, regression cases, and exact test result. Work only in this directory.

muse-wonder/02-bug-fixing/test_ttl_cache.py

import unittest

from ttl_cache import TTLCache


class Clock:
    def __init__(self):
        self.now = 100.0

    def __call__(self):
        return self.now


class TTLCacheTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.clock = Clock()

    def test_round_trip(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")

    def test_expires_at_boundary(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 10
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_get_refreshes_lru(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.get("a")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")

    def test_falsy_values_are_returned(self):
        cache = TTLCache(4, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("zero", 0)
        cache.put("false", False)
        cache.put("none", None)
        cache.put("empty", "")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("zero"), 0)
        self.assertIs(cache.get("false"), False)
        self.assertIsNone(cache.get("none"))
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("empty"), "")

    def test_missing_key_raises_key_error(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("missing")

    def test_expired_entry_raises_and_is_removed(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 11
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 0)

    def test_get_does_not_refresh_ttl(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 6
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.clock.now += 5
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_put_updates_value_timestamp_and_recency(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        self.clock.now += 6
        cache.put("a", "A2")
        # "a" is now most recently used, so inserting "c" evicts "b".
        cache.put("c", "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")
        # Timestamp was refreshed by the second put: 6s later still live
        # (12s after the original put would have expired).
        self.clock.now += 6
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")

    def test_put_existing_key_does_not_evict(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("a", "A2")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")

    def test_expired_entries_removed_before_capacity_eviction(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 6
        cache.put("b", "B")
        # Make "a" most recently used; "b" becomes the LRU live entry.
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.clock.now += 5
        # Now "a" is expired (age 11), "b" is live (age 5) but LRU.
        # Purging expired first must keep "b" when inserting "c".
        cache.put("c", "C")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_capacity_evicts_lru_live_entry(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 100, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")

    def test_invalid_max_size_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(0, 10, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(-1, 10, self.clock)

    def test_invalid_ttl_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(2, 0, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(2, -5, self.clock)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

muse-wonder/02-bug-fixing/ttl_cache.py

import time
from collections import OrderedDict


class TTLCache:
    def __init__(self, max_size, ttl_seconds, clock=time.monotonic):
        if max_size < 1:
            raise ValueError("max_size must be at least 1")
        if ttl_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError("ttl_seconds must be greater than 0")
        self.max_size = max_size
        self.ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
        self.clock = clock
        self._items = OrderedDict()

    def _purge_expired(self, now):
        expired = [
            key
            for key, (_, created) in self._items.items()
            if now - created >= self.ttl_seconds
        ]
        for key in expired:
            del self._items[key]

    def put(self, key, value):
        now = self.clock()
        self._purge_expired(now)
        if key in self._items:
            self._items[key] = (value, now)
            self._items.move_to_end(key)
            return
        while len(self._items) >= self.max_size:
            self._items.popitem(last=False)
        self._items[key] = (value, now)

    def get(self, key):
        try:
            value, created = self._items[key]
        except KeyError:
            raise KeyError(key) from None
        now = self.clock()
        if now - created >= self.ttl_seconds:
            del self._items[key]
            raise KeyError(key)
        self._items.move_to_end(key)
        return value

    def __len__(self):
        self._purge_expired(self.clock())
        return len(self._items)

muse-wonder-full/02-bug-fixing/RESPONSE.md

Task 02 — TTL/LRU cache bug fixing

Public API was not changed: TTLCache(max_size, ttl_seconds, clock=time.monotonic), put(key, value), get(key), and __len__.

Root causes and fixes

  1. Wrong clock in put

    • Root cause: put stored time.time() (wall time) while get compared it against the injected self.clock() (monotonic / fake test clock). Ages were computed across two different time bases (e.g. fake clock at 100.0 vs a wall timestamp), so entries effectively never expired with an injected clock.
    • Fix: put now stores now = self.clock().

  2. Falsy values rejected in get

    • Root cause: if not value: raise KeyError(key) treated stored 0, False, None, "", etc. as missing.
    • Fix: removed the truthiness check. get raises KeyError only when the key is absent or the entry is expired, and otherwise returns the stored value as-is.

  3. Wrong expiry boundary

    • Root cause: expiry used age > ttl_seconds, so an entry at exactly age == ttl_seconds was incorrectly still live.
    • Fix: expiry is now - created >= ttl_seconds (via _is_expired).

  4. get did not refresh LRU recency

    • Root cause: get returned the value without reordering the OrderedDict.
    • Fix: a successful get calls self._items.move_to_end(key) and does not touch the stored timestamp, so recency is refreshed but TTL is not.

  5. put on an existing key did not update recency and could evict spuriously

    • Root cause: OrderedDict.__setitem__ on an existing key keeps its old position, so recency was not refreshed. Also the capacity check (len >= max_size) ran before the insert unconditionally, so updating an existing key in a full cache evicted the LRU entry even though the size would not grow.
    • Fix: after pruning, if the key already exists, put updates its (value, now) timestamp, calls move_to_end(key), and returns without any eviction.

  6. Expired entries were not removed before capacity enforcement

    • Root cause: put evicted the LRU entry whenever the cache was full, even if some other entry was already expired — a live LRU entry could be evicted while an expired entry was retained.
    • Fix: put first calls _prune_expired(now) to remove every entry with age >= ttl_seconds, then evicts popitem(last=False) (least recently used live entry) only while still at/over capacity for a new key.

  7. No argument validation

    • Root cause: __init__ accepted any max_size / ttl_seconds.
    • Fix: max_size < 1 raises ValueError; ttl_seconds <= 0 raises ValueError.

  8. Expired entries lingered (cleanup)

    • Fix: an expired entry is deleted when get finds it expired, and __len__ prunes expired entries before counting, so length reflects live entries.

Regression cases added

Added to test_ttl_cache.py (original 3 tests kept unchanged; 10 new tests):

  • test_falsy_values_are_returned — 0, False, None, "" round-trip.
  • test_missing_key_raises_key_error — absent key raises KeyError.
  • test_not_expired_just_before_boundary — age 9.999 with TTL 10 is live (complements the existing test_expires_at_boundary at exactly age 10).
  • test_get_does_not_refresh_ttl — get at age 9, then expiry at original age 10.
  • test_put_updates_value_timestamp_and_recency — re-put changes value, resets TTL (live 9s after update / 18s after first insert, expired at 10s after update), and makes the key most-recent so a later insert evicts the other key.
  • test_put_update_when_full_does_not_evict_other_key — updating a key in a full cache keeps both keys and len == 2.
  • test_expired_entries_removed_before_capacity_eviction — expired most-recent entry is pruned instead of evicting the live LRU entry when inserting into a full cache.
  • test_capacity_eviction_removes_lru_live_entry — plain LRU eviction.
  • test_invalid_max_size_raises_value_error — max_size of 0 and -1.
  • test_invalid_ttl_raises_value_error — ttl_seconds of 0 and -1.

muse-wonder-full/02-bug-fixing/TASK.md

Task 02 — Bug fixing: TTL/LRU cache

ttl_cache.py is a deliberately faulty implementation. Fix it without changing the public API and add regression tests.

Required behavior:

  • get(key) returns the stored value, including falsy values such as 0, False and None; it raises KeyError only for missing or expired entries.
  • TTL uses the injected monotonic clock and expires at age >= ttl_seconds.
  • A successful get refreshes LRU recency but does not refresh TTL.
  • put updates an existing key's value, timestamp and recency.
  • Before enforcing capacity, expired entries are removed.
  • Capacity eviction removes the least recently used live entry.
  • max_size < 1 and ttl_seconds <= 0 raise ValueError.

Run the tests. Write RESPONSE.md with the root causes, fixes, regression cases, and exact test result. Work only in this directory.

muse-wonder-full/02-bug-fixing/test_ttl_cache.py

import unittest

from ttl_cache import TTLCache


class Clock:
    def __init__(self):
        self.now = 100.0

    def __call__(self):
        return self.now


class TTLCacheTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.clock = Clock()

    def test_round_trip(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")

    def test_expires_at_boundary(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 10
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_get_refreshes_lru(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.get("a")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")

    # --- Regression tests ---

    def test_falsy_values_are_returned(self):
        cache = TTLCache(4, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("zero", 0)
        cache.put("false", False)
        cache.put("none", None)
        cache.put("empty", "")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("zero"), 0)
        self.assertIs(cache.get("false"), False)
        self.assertIsNone(cache.get("none"))
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("empty"), "")

    def test_missing_key_raises_key_error(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("missing")

    def test_not_expired_just_before_boundary(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 9.999
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")

    def test_get_does_not_refresh_ttl(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 9
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.clock.now += 1
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_put_updates_value_timestamp_and_recency(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        self.clock.now += 9
        cache.put("a", "A2")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)
        # Recency: "a" is now most recent, so inserting "c" evicts "b".
        cache.put("c", "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")
        # Timestamp: 9s after the update (18s after first put) still live.
        self.clock.now += 9
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")
        self.clock.now += 1
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_put_update_when_full_does_not_evict_other_key(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 100, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("a", "A2")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")

    def test_expired_entries_removed_before_capacity_eviction(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")  # t=100
        self.clock.now += 5
        cache.put("b", "B")  # t=105
        cache.get("a")  # "a" most-recent, "b" is LRU
        self.clock.now += 5  # t=110: "a" expired (age 10), "b" live (age 5)
        cache.put("c", "C")
        # Expired "a" must be pruned instead of evicting live LRU "b".
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)

    def test_capacity_eviction_removes_lru_live_entry(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 100, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")

    def test_invalid_max_size_raises_value_error(self):
        for bad in (0, -1):
            with self.subTest(max_size=bad):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    TTLCache(bad, 10, self.clock)

    def test_invalid_ttl_raises_value_error(self):
        for bad in (0, -1):
            with self.subTest(ttl_seconds=bad):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    TTLCache(2, bad, self.clock)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

muse-wonder-full/02-bug-fixing/ttl_cache.py

import time
from collections import OrderedDict


class TTLCache:
    def __init__(self, max_size, ttl_seconds, clock=time.monotonic):
        if max_size < 1:
            raise ValueError("max_size must be at least 1")
        if ttl_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError("ttl_seconds must be greater than 0")
        self.max_size = max_size
        self.ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
        self.clock = clock
        self._items = OrderedDict()

    def _is_expired(self, created, now):
        return now - created >= self.ttl_seconds

    def _prune_expired(self, now=None):
        if now is None:
            now = self.clock()
        expired = [
            key
            for key, (_value, created) in self._items.items()
            if self._is_expired(created, now)
        ]
        for key in expired:
            del self._items[key]

    def put(self, key, value):
        now = self.clock()
        self._prune_expired(now)
        if key in self._items:
            self._items[key] = (value, now)
            self._items.move_to_end(key)
            return
        while len(self._items) >= self.max_size:
            self._items.popitem(last=False)
        self._items[key] = (value, now)

    def get(self, key):
        try:
            value, created = self._items[key]
        except KeyError:
            raise KeyError(key) from None
        if self._is_expired(created, self.clock()):
            del self._items[key]
            raise KeyError(key)
        self._items.move_to_end(key)
        return value

    def __len__(self):
        self._prune_expired()
        return len(self._items)

mimo/02-bug-fixing/RESPONSE.md

Response — Task 02: TTL/LRU cache bug fixes

Root causes

  1. Falsy values treated as missing — get used if not value: raise KeyError, so stored 0, False, None, and "" were wrongly rejected.
  2. Wall-clock timestamps — put recorded time.time() instead of the injected clock, so TTL checks mixed two time bases and the boundary test could never expire entries.
  3. Off-by-one expiry — comparison used age > ttl_seconds; spec requires expiry at age >= ttl_seconds.
  4. get did not refresh LRU recency — successful gets left the entry at its old position, so the wrong item was later evicted under capacity pressure.
  5. put evicted before key-existence check — updating an existing key while at capacity popped the LRU entry even though no growth occurred.
  6. put did not purge expired entries first — dead entries counted against capacity and could force eviction of live entries.
  7. put did not move updated keys to the MRU position.
  8. No constructor validation — max_size < 1 and ttl_seconds <= 0 were accepted.

Fixes (public API unchanged)

  • get returns the stored value as-is (including falsy values); raises KeyError only for missing or expired keys (expired entry is also dropped from the map).
  • All timestamps come from self.clock(); expiry is now - created >= ttl_seconds.
  • Successful get calls move_to_end (LRU refresh) and never rewrites the timestamp (TTL is not extended).
  • put purges expired entries, evicts the LRU live entry only when inserting a new key at capacity, then stores (value, self.clock()) and move_to_ends the key (covers both insert and update recency/timestamp refresh).
  • __init__ raises ValueError for max_size < 1 or ttl_seconds <= 0.

Regression cases (test_ttl_cache_regression.py)

Case Behavior pinned
test_get_returns_falsy_values 0, False, None, "" round-trip
test_missing_key_raises_key_error missing key → KeyError
test_expired_raises_key_error age == ttl → KeyError
test_not_expired_just_before_boundary age < ttl still live
test_ttl_uses_injected_clock expiry driven only by injected clock
test_get_refreshes_lru_not_ttl get moves recency, does not extend TTL
test_get_refreshes_lru_for_eviction get prevents the touched entry from being evicted
test_put_updates_value_timestamp_and_recency put refreshes value, timestamp, and recency
test_put_existing_key_at_capacity_does_not_evict update at capacity keeps all entries
test_expired_removed_before_capacity expired entries purged before capacity logic
test_capacity_evicts_lru_live_entry eviction picks least-recently-used live entry
test_invalid_max_size / test_invalid_ttl ValueError on max_size < 1, ttl_seconds <= 0

Test result

Ran 16 tests in 0.001s

OK

(3 original tests in test_ttl_cache.py + 13 regression tests in test_ttl_cache_regression.py, python3 -m unittest test_ttl_cache test_ttl_cache_regression -v.)

import unittest

from ttl_cache import TTLCache


class Clock:
    def __init__(self):
        self.now = 100.0

    def __call__(self):
        return self.now


class TTLCacheRegressionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.clock = Clock()

    def test_get_returns_falsy_values(self):
        cache = TTLCache(4, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("zero", 0)
        cache.put("false", False)
        cache.put("none", None)
        cache.put("empty", "")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("zero"), 0)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("false"), False)
        self.assertIsNone(cache.get("none"))
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("empty"), "")

    def test_missing_key_raises_key_error(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("missing")

    def test_expired_raises_key_error(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 10
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_not_expired_just_before_boundary(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 9.999
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")

    def test_ttl_uses_injected_clock(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 5
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.clock.now += 5
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_get_refreshes_lru_not_ttl(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 5
        cache.put("b", "B")
        self.clock.now += 4
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.clock.now += 2
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")

    def test_get_refreshes_lru_for_eviction(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")

    def test_put_updates_value_timestamp_and_recency(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        self.clock.now += 5
        cache.put("a", "A2")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")
        self.clock.now += 5
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("a", "A3")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A3")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("b")

    def test_put_existing_key_at_capacity_does_not_evict(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("a", "A2")
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 2)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("a"), "A2")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")

    def test_expired_removed_before_capacity(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("old1", 1)
        cache.put("old2", 2)
        self.clock.now += 10
        cache.put("new", 3)
        self.assertEqual(len(cache), 1)
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("new"), 3)
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("old1")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("old2")

    def test_capacity_evicts_lru_live_entry(self):
        cache = TTLCache(2, 10, self.clock)
        cache.put("a", "A")
        cache.put("b", "B")
        cache.put("c", "C")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            cache.get("a")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("b"), "B")
        self.assertEqual(cache.get("c"), "C")

    def test_invalid_max_size(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(0, 10, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(-1, 10, self.clock)

    def test_invalid_ttl(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(2, 0, self.clock)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            TTLCache(2, -5, self.clock)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

mimo/02-bug-fixing/ttl_cache.py

import time
from collections import OrderedDict


class TTLCache:
    def __init__(self, max_size, ttl_seconds, clock=time.monotonic):
        if max_size < 1:
            raise ValueError("max_size must be >= 1")
        if ttl_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError("ttl_seconds must be > 0")
        self.max_size = max_size
        self.ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
        self.clock = clock
        self._items = OrderedDict()

    def _expired(self, created, now):
        return now - created >= self.ttl_seconds

    def _purge_expired(self):
        now = self.clock()
        stale = [k for k, (_, created) in self._items.items()
                 if self._expired(created, now)]
        for k in stale:
            del self._items[k]

    def put(self, key, value):
        self._purge_expired()
        if key not in self._items and len(self._items) >= self.max_size:
            self._items.popitem(last=False)
        self._items[key] = (value, self.clock())
        self._items.move_to_end(key)

    def get(self, key):
        value, created = self._items[key]
        if self._expired(created, self.clock()):
            del self._items[key]
            raise KeyError(key)
        self._items.move_to_end(key)
        return value

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self._items)

kimi-k3/02-bug-fixing/RESPONSE.md

Task 02 — Bug fixing: TTL/LRU cache

Root causes

  1. Falsy values rejected by get. get contained if not value: raise KeyError(key), so legitimately stored values such as 0, False and None could never be read back.
  2. Wrong clock in put. Timestamps were recorded with time.time() while get compared against the injected clock, making TTL behavior inconsistent/untestable.
  3. Wrong expiry comparison. get used > instead of >=, so entries survived exactly at age == ttl_seconds instead of expiring at the boundary.
  4. No LRU recency maintenance. get never moved the entry to the most-recently-used end (OrderedDict.move_to_end), and put on an existing key kept its original insertion position and timestamp, so eviction order and TTL refresh on update were broken.
  5. No expired-entry purge before capacity enforcement. put evicted the oldest entry purely by position without first removing expired entries, so live entries could be evicted while dead ones occupied capacity.
  6. put on an existing key triggered eviction. The capacity check ran before checking whether the key already existed, so an update could evict an unrelated entry.
  7. Missing argument validation. max_size < 1 and ttl_seconds <= 0 were accepted silently instead of raising ValueError.

Fixes

  • Constructor now raises ValueError for max_size < 1 or ttl_seconds <= 0.
  • put records timestamps with the injected clock; updating an existing key replaces the value and timestamp and moves it to the MRU end without any eviction.
  • put (new key) purges expired entries via _purge_expired() before enforcing capacity, then evicts the LRU entry (popitem(last=False)) only if still full.
  • get raises KeyError only for missing keys or entries whose clock() - created >= ttl_seconds (expired entries are deleted on access), returns any stored value including falsy ones, and calls move_to_end to refresh recency without touching the TTL timestamp.
  • Public API (__init__, put, get, __len__) is unchanged.

Regression tests added (test_ttl_cache.py)

  • test_get_returns_falsy_values — 0, False, None round-trip through get.
  • test_uses_injected_clock — expiry follows the injected clock, boundary inclusive.
  • test_get_refreshes_lru_but_not_ttl — a get mid-life does not extend the TTL.
  • test_put_updates_value_timestamp_and_recency — update resets value, TTL and LRU order.
  • test_put_existing_key_does_not_evict — update never evicts other entries.
  • test_expired_removed_before_capacity_eviction — purge happens before eviction.
  • test_expired_purge_preserves_live_lru_order — live entries survive the purge.
  • test_capacity_evicts_lru_live_entry — eviction picks the least recently used live entry.
  • test_invalid_arguments_raise_value_error — max_size < 1, ttl_seconds <= 0.
  • test_missing_key_raises_key_error — absent keys raise KeyError.

Exact test result
